## P2-老20 · An item you spent more than expected on — Muye 的自行车（Object）

> **Cue**: 是什么 / 花了多少 / 为何买 / 为何超支 · 题型 Object

### 🎤 你的实产版（6/27 session 练过 · 你的词/结构，未降未改）

> 你自己练过的一整套：P2 = 3D 打印机（你的真实版，同 cue、不同物）；P3 = 你当天练的 6 题（钱/经济域）。**不降档**，只加英式中文珠子。

**P2（你的版 · 3D 打印机）**

| 句 | 英式中文珠子 | 你的实产英文 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | 我 花 得 比 预期 多的 那件 东西 是 一台 3D 打印机。 | The item I spent more than expected on is a 3D printer. |
| 2 | 一开始，我 只 想 买 一台 入门级 的，但 最后 我 买 了 一台 高级 的。 | At first, I only wanted to buy an entry-level one, but in the end I bought an advanced one. |
| 3 | 它 花 了 超过 4000 块，（这）几乎 是 我 预算 的 两倍。 | It cost more than 4,000 yuan, which was almost double my budget. |
| 4 | 我 入 这个 坑 是 通过 我 一个 同事，（他 是 一个 真正的 发烧友）——他 甚至 用 零件 造 了 他 自己的 机器。 | I got into it through one of my colleagues, who's a real enthusiast — he even built his own machine from spare parts. |
| 5 | 他 给 我 看 他的 能 做 什么，而 我 立刻 就 上瘾了。 | He showed me what his could do, and I was hooked straight away. |
| 6 | 有 几个 原因 我 买 它。 | There are a couple of reasons I bought it. |
| 7 | 首先，它 能 打印 各种 玩具——五彩的 恐龙、乐高 积木、那种 东西。 | First, it can print all sorts of toys — colourful dinosaurs, Lego blocks, that kind of thing. |
| 8 | 除此之外，它 是 一个 偷偷的 方式、[去]让 我 儿子 对 工程 感兴趣——他 能 看到 屏幕上 的 一个 东西 怎么 变成 一个 真的 物体。 | On top of that, it's a sneaky way to get my son interested in engineering — he can see how something on the screen turns into a real object. |
| 9 | 至于 为什么 我 花 得 比 预期 多，那 主要 是 因为 那些 配件——尤其 一个 叫 AMS 的 部件，（它 让 它 能 打印 多种 颜色）。 | As for why I spent more than I expected, it was mainly because of the add-ons — especially a part called AMS, which lets it print in multiple colours. |
| 10 | 但 说实话，整 件 事 是 值得的。 | But honestly, the whole thing was worth it. |
| 11 | 周末，我 儿子 坐 在 我 旁边，我们 一起 设计 某个 东西，然后 看 那 打印机 一层一层 把 它 造 出来。 | On weekends, my son sits right next to me, we design something together, and watch the printer build it layer by layer. |

> The item I spent more than expected on is a 3D printer. At first, I only wanted to buy an entry-level one, but in the end I bought an advanced one. It cost more than 4,000 yuan, which was almost double my budget. I got into it through one of my colleagues, who's a real enthusiast — he even built his own machine from spare parts. He showed me what his could do, and I was hooked straight away. There are a couple of reasons I bought it. First, it can print all sorts of toys — colourful dinosaurs, Lego blocks, that kind of thing. On top of that, it's a sneaky way to get my son interested in engineering — he can see how something on the screen turns into a real object. As for why I spent more than I expected, it was mainly because of the add-ons — especially a part called AMS, which lets it print in multiple colours. But honestly, the whole thing was worth it. On weekends, my son sits right next to me, we design something together, and watch the printer build it layer by layer.

**P3（你练的 6 题 · 钱/经济）**

**你Q1. Do you often buy more than you expected?**

| 句 | 英式中文珠子 | 你的实产英文 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | 不太，说实话——我 自己 几乎 不 买 任何 东西，网上 或 店里。 | Not really, to be honest — I barely buy anything myself, online or in shops. |
| 2 | 我 老婆 打理 那 一切，不管 是 家用的 东西 还是 个人的 东西。 | My wife takes care of all of it, whether it's household stuff or personal things. |
| 3 | 说句公道话，当 我 确实 买 某个 东西，我 通常 超 预算，因为 我 总是 想要 那 最好的。 | To be fair, when I do buy something, I usually go over budget, because I always want the best one. |

> Not really, to be honest — I barely buy anything myself, online or in shops. My wife takes care of all of it, whether it's household stuff or personal things. To be fair, when I do buy something, I usually go over budget, because I always want the best one.

**你Q2. What do young people spend their money on?**

| 句 | 英式中文珠子 | 你的实产英文 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | 主要 是 房租，我会说。 | Mainly rent, I'd say. |
| 2 | 这些天 房租 一直 在 涨，尤其 在 大 城市，所以 它 吃 掉 他们 工资 的 一 大块。 | These days rent keeps going up, especially in big cities, so it eats up a big chunk of their salary. |
| 3 | 除此之外，还有 那些 最新的 电子产品——手机、笔记本、那种 东西——（那 也 不 便宜）。 | On top of that, there's the latest gadgets — phones, laptops, that kind of thing — which don't come cheap either. |
| 4 | 所以 总的来说，它 大多 是 住房 和 他们 每天 用的 东西。 | So overall, it's mostly housing and the stuff they use every day. |

> Mainly rent, I'd say. These days rent keeps going up, especially in big cities, so it eats up a big chunk of their salary. On top of that, there's the latest gadgets — phones, laptops, that kind of thing — which don't come cheap either. So overall, it's mostly housing and the stuff they use every day.

**你Q3. Is it important to save money?**

| 句 | 英式中文珠子 | 你的实产英文 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | 绝对，我 觉得 它 真的 关键——哪怕 只 一点点。 | Absolutely, I think it's really key — even just a little. |
| 2 | 这些天 有 一大堆 东西 需要 钱——房租、医疗、这一切。 | These days there are loads of things that need money — rent, healthcare, all of it. |
| 3 | 存 一点 起来 并不 真的 拘束 你的 生活；要说的话，它 给 你 一个 缓冲、当 某个 东西 出 问题 的时候。 | Putting a bit aside doesn't really cramp your life; if anything, it gives you a cushion when something goes wrong. |
| 4 | 话虽如此，你 不该 过头——存钱 是 一个 工具，不是 那 目标。 | That said, you shouldn't overdo it — saving's a tool, not the goal. |
| 5 | 钱 在 那儿 是 用来 花的，不是 只 攥 着。 | Money's there to be used, not just sat on. |

> Absolutely, I think it's really key — even just a little. These days there are loads of things that need money — rent, healthcare, all of it. Putting a bit aside doesn't really cramp your life; if anything, it gives you a cushion when something goes wrong. That said, you shouldn't overdo it — saving's a tool, not the goal. Money's there to be used, not just sat on.

**你Q4. Do people buy things they don't need?**

| 句 | 英式中文珠子 | 你的实产英文 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | 一直，我 觉得。 | Always, I think. |
| 2 | 那 主要 原因 是 冲动——很多 购买 是 被 情绪 驱动，不是 真 需要。 | The main reason is impulse — a lot of buying is driven by emotion, not real need. |
| 3 | 这些天 广告 到处 都是，而 当 人们 看到 一个，尤其 有 打折 的时候，他们 生出 一股 强烈的 购买 冲动，即使 他们 并不 真的 需要 它。 | These days ads are everywhere, and when people see one, especially with a sale on, they get a strong urge to buy, even if they don't really need it. |
| 4 | 在 今天 这个 快节奏的 世界，人们 甚至 不 慢下来 问 一下 他们 是否 真的 需要 某个 东西。 | In today's fast-paced world, folk don't even slow down to ask if they actually need something. |
| 5 | 所以 广告 真的 很 擅长 让 想要 感觉 像 需要。 | So advertising's really good at making wants feel like needs. |

> Always, I think. The main reason is impulse — a lot of buying is driven by emotion, not real need. These days ads are everywhere, and when people see one, especially with a sale on, they get a strong urge to buy, even if they don't really need it. In today's fast-paced world, folk don't even slow down to ask if they actually need something. So advertising's really good at making wants feel like needs.

**你Q5. Is it the rich people's responsibility to donate money?**

| 句 | 英式中文珠子 | 你的实产英文 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | 我 不 这么 觉得——至少 不 作为 一个 义务。 | I don't think so — at least not as a duty. |
| 2 | 帮 有需要的 人 真的 是 那 政府 的 活，不 归 富有的 个人。 | Helping people in need is really the government's job, not down to wealthy individuals. |
| 3 | 能够 帮忙 并不 让 它 成为 他们的 义务。 | Being able to help doesn't make it their duty. |
| 4 | 当然，如果 富人 回馈 更多 会 很好，但 那 应该 通过 像 税 这样的 东西 来，而不是 靠 强加 在 他们 身上。 | Of course, it'd be great if the rich gave more back, but that should come through things like taxes, not by forcing it on them. |
| 5 | 所以 我会说 鼓励 它，别 强迫 它——慷慨 该 发自 内心，不是 出于 压力。 | So I'd say encourage it, don't force it — generosity should come from the heart, not from pressure. |

> I don't think so — at least not as a duty. Helping people in need is really the government's job, not down to wealthy individuals. Being able to help doesn't make it their duty. Of course, it'd be great if the rich gave more back, but that should come through things like taxes, not by forcing it on them. So I'd say encourage it, don't force it — generosity should come from the heart, not from pressure.

**你Q6. What kind of things do people pay a high price for?**

| 句 | 英式中文珠子 | 你的实产英文 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | 主要 是 他们的 爱好 和 健康，我会说。 | Mainly their hobbies and health, I'd say. |
| 2 | 人们 倾向于 付 多得多、为 他们 真正 热爱的——对 一个 吃货，那 是 一顿 花哨的 饭；对 一个 游戏玩家，一台 顶配的 电脑。 | People tend to pay much more for what they really love — for a foodie, that's a fancy meal; for a gamer, a top-end PC. |
| 3 | 他们 把 它 看作 花得 值的 钱，不是 一个 浪费。 | They see it as money well spent, not a waste. |
| 4 | 至于 健康，你 真的 没法 给 它 定 一个 价——没有 它，别的 一切 都 无所谓。 | As for health, you can't really put a price on it — without it, nothing else matters. |
| 5 | 所以 人们 会 乐意 大手花钱 在 一辆 可靠的 车、一张 健身房 会员、或 好的 食物 上。 | So people will happily splash out on a reliable car, a gym membership, or good food. |

> Mainly their hobbies and health, I'd say. People tend to pay much more for what they really love — for a foodie, that's a fancy meal; for a gamer, a top-end PC. They see it as money well spent, not a waste. As for health, you can't really put a price on it — without it, nothing else matters. So people will happily splash out on a reliable car, a gym membership, or good food.

---

### ①P2 拆解（英式中文珠子 → 范文英文）

| 句/功能 | 英式中文珠子 | 范文英文 |
|---|---|---|
| [opener] | 那么，我想聊的那件 东西 是 一辆 儿童 自行车、（我 为 我 五岁的 儿子 Muye 买的）。 | So, the item I'd like to talk about is a children's bicycle I bought for my five-year-old son, Muye. |
| [cost] | 我 进去时 预期 花 几百 块钱，但 我 走出来时 已经 花 了 远远 超过 一千。 | I'd gone in expecting to spend a few hundred yuan, but I walked out having spent well over a thousand. |
| [why] | 我 买 它 的原因 简单——Muye 一直 求 我 教 他 骑，而 我 想 让 他 户外 活跃、而不是 粘 在 一个 屏幕上。 | The reason I got it was simple — Muye kept begging me to teach him to ride, and I wanted to get him active outdoors instead of glued to a screen. |
| [why over·提问] | 那 为什么 它 花 得 比 预期 多？ | So why did it cost more than expected? |
| [why over] | 嗯，那 车 本身 还行，但 是 所有 那些 附加件 堆 起来了。 | Well, the bike itself was fine, but it was all the add-ons that piled up. |
| [why over·细] | 我是说，一个 像样的 头盔、护膝 和 护肘、一把 结实的 锁、一个 小 篮子——在 我 反应过来 之前，我妻子 和 我 也 已经 升级 到 一个 造得 更好的 型号，因为 我 不 想 在 他的 安全上 偷工减料。 | I mean, a proper helmet, knee and elbow pads, a sturdy lock, a little basket — before I knew it, my wife and I had also upgraded to a better-built model, because I didn't want to cut corners on his safety. |
| [EL] | 到头来，有 一个 孩子 你 倾向于 花 更多、甚至 没 意识到 它。 | At the end of the day, with a kid you tend to spend more without even realising it. |
| [EL·细] | 但 你 一分钱 一分货，对吧？ | But you get what you pay for, right? |
| [closer] | 所以，对，没 后悔。 | So yeah, no regrets. |

### ②P2 整段（shadow）

> So, the item I'd like to talk about is a children's bicycle I bought for my five-year-old son, Muye. I'd gone in expecting to spend a few hundred yuan, but I walked out having spent well over a thousand. The reason I got it was simple — Muye kept begging me to teach him to ride, and I wanted to get him active outdoors instead of glued to a screen. So why did it cost more than expected? Well, the bike itself was fine, but it was all the add-ons that piled up. I mean, a proper helmet, knee and elbow pads, a sturdy lock, a little basket — before I knew it, my wife and I had also upgraded to a better-built model, because I didn't want to cut corners on his safety. At the end of the day, with a kid you tend to spend more without even realising it. But you get what you pay for, right? So yeah, no regrets.

### 句型/模板（可搬走）

- **item opener + relative后置**：一辆我为五岁儿子买的儿童自行车 → `a children's bicycle I bought for my five-year-old son`
- **分词状语对比**：进去时预期花几百，走出来已花超过一千 → `I'd gone in expecting to spend …, but I walked out having spent well over a thousand`
- **一直求 + instead of**：Muye一直求我教他骑；想让他户外活跃而不是粘屏幕 → `kept begging me to teach him to ride` / `get him active outdoors instead of glued to a screen`
- **前置提问**：那为什么它花得比预期多？ → `So why did it cost more than expected?`
- **强调句 it was … that**：是所有那些附加件堆起来了 → `it was all the add-ons that piled up`
- **before I knew it + 过去完成**：还没反应过来我们已升级到更好型号 → `before I knew it, … had also upgraded to a better-built model`
- **金句**：有孩子你会不知不觉花更多；一分钱一分货 → `with a kid you tend to spend more without even realising it` / `you get what you pay for`

---

### ①P3 拆解 + ②整段

**Q1. Do you often buy more than you expected?**

| 句 | 英式中文珠子 | 范文英文 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | 说实话，比 我 愿意 承认的 更 常。 | Honestly, more often than I'd like to admit. |
| 2 | 我 通常 带 一个 清晰的 预算 进去，但 然后 我 会 瞥见 一点 额外的、（那种 抓住 我 眼球的），而 在 我 反应过来 之前 我 已经 超了。 | I usually go in with a clear budget, but then I'll spot a little extra that catches my eye, and before I know it I've gone over. |
| 3 | 比如，买 杂货 总是 最后 花 得 更多，因为 诱人的 零食 就 不知怎么 溜 进 那 篮子。 | For instance, shopping for groceries always ends up costing more, because tempting snacks just sneak into the basket somehow. |

> Honestly, more often than I'd like to admit. I usually go in with a clear budget, but then I'll spot a little extra that catches my eye, and before I know it I've gone over. For instance, shopping for groceries always ends up costing more, because tempting snacks just sneak into the basket somehow.

**Q2. What do you think young people spend most of their money on?**

| 句 | 英式中文珠子 | 范文英文 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | 我会说 它 大多 在 社交的 东西上，比如 出去吃、咖啡、和 跟 朋友 去 演唱会 或 电影。 | I'd say it's mostly on social stuff, like eating out, coffee, and going to concerts or films with friends. |
| 2 | 他们 在 一个 年纪、（那里 体验 比 存钱 更 要紧），所以 那 就是 钱 去 的地方。 | They're at an age where experiences matter more than saving, so that's where the money goes. |
| 3 | 此外，他们 很多 在 小设备 和 衣服上 大手花钱，因为 看起来 时髦 对 他们 感觉 挺 重要。 | Plus, a lot of them splash out on gadgets and clothes, since looking trendy feels pretty important to them. |

> I'd say it's mostly on social stuff, like eating out, coffee, and going to concerts or films with friends. They're at an age where experiences matter more than saving, so that's where the money goes. Plus, a lot of them splash out on gadgets and clothes, since looking trendy feels pretty important to them.

**Q3. Do you think it is important to save money? Why?**

| 句 | 英式中文珠子 | 范文英文 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | 绝对，我 觉得 它 真的 关键。 | Absolutely, I think it's really key. |
| 2 | 生活 朝 你 扔 惊吓——一场 突然的 病 或 丢 一份 工作——而 有 一些 存款 藏 着 给 你 一个 真正的 安全网。 | Life throws surprises at you — a sudden illness or losing a job — and having some savings tucked away gives you a real safety net. |
| 3 | 比如，某人、（没有 缓冲的）可能 最后 以 高 利息 借钱。 | For instance, someone with no buffer might end up borrowing at high interest. |
| 4 | 所以 现在 一点 自律 省 后来 很多 压力。 | So a bit of discipline now saves a lot of stress later. |

> Absolutely, I think it's really key. Life throws surprises at you — a sudden illness or losing a job — and having some savings tucked away gives you a real safety net. For instance, someone with no buffer might end up borrowing at high interest. So a bit of discipline now saves a lot of stress later.

**Q4. Do people buy things they don't need?**

| 句 | 英式中文珠子 | 范文英文 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | 哦，一直，说实话。 | Oh, all the time, honestly. |
| 2 | 很多 购买 是 被 情绪 驱动、而不是 真 需要——人们 抓 某种东西 [去]让 自己 高兴 或 因为 它 在 打折。 | A lot of buying is driven by emotion rather than real need — people grab something to cheer themselves up or because it's on sale. |
| 3 | 比如，人们 拥有的 一半 小设备 一周 后 几乎 不 被 用。 | For instance, half the gadgets folk own barely get used after a week. |
| 4 | 所以，对，广告 真的 擅长 让 想要 感觉 像 需要。 | So yeah, advertising's really good at making wants feel like needs. |

> Oh, all the time, honestly. A lot of buying is driven by emotion rather than real need — people grab something to cheer themselves up or because it's on sale. For instance, half the gadgets folk own barely get used after a week. So yeah, advertising's really good at making wants feel like needs.

**Q5. Do you think it is the rich people's responsibility to donate money to people in need?**

| 句 | 英式中文珠子 | 范文英文 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | 说句公道话，我 不会 把它 叫做 一个 严格的 义务，但 我 确实 觉得 那些 富人 在 一个 很好的 位置 [去]帮忙。 | To be fair, I wouldn't call it a strict duty, but I do think the wealthy are in a great position to help. |
| 2 | 他们 能 给、而 不 真的 伤 到 他们，而 它 为 别人 树立 一个 好 榜样。 | They can give without it really hurting them, and it sets a good example for others. |
| 3 | 话虽如此，慷慨 该 发自 内心、不是 出于 压力，否则 它 感觉 空洞。 | Having said that, generosity should come from the heart, not from pressure, otherwise it feels hollow. |

> To be fair, I wouldn't call it a strict duty, but I do think the wealthy are in a great position to help. They can give without it really hurting them, and it sets a good example for others. Having said that, generosity should come from the heart, not from pressure, otherwise it feels hollow.

**Q6. What kind of things are people happy to pay a high price for?**

| 句 | 英式中文珠子 | 范文英文 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | 我觉得 人们 乐意 付 大价钱、为 无论 什么 他们 真心 热爱的。 | I reckon people happily pay top dollar for whatever they're truly passionate about. |
| 2 | 对 一个 吃货，那 是 一顿 花哨的 饭；对 一个 旅行者，一个 梦想的 假期。 | For a foodie, that's a fancy meal; for a traveller, a dream holiday. |
| 3 | 他们 把 它 看作 花得 值的 钱，不是 一个 浪费。 | They see it as money well spent, not a waste. |
| 4 | 此外，人们 乐意 在 健康 和 安全上 大手花钱，比如 一辆 可靠的 车，因为 你 真的 没法 给 内心的 安宁 定 一个 价。 | Plus, folk gladly splash out on health and safety, like a reliable car, since you can't really put a price on peace of mind. |

> I reckon people happily pay top dollar for whatever they're truly passionate about. For a foodie, that's a fancy meal; for a traveller, a dream holiday. They see it as money well spent, not a waste. Plus, folk gladly splash out on health and safety, like a reliable car, since you can't really put a price on peace of mind.

**P3 句型/模板**：`more often than I'd like to admit` · `a little extra that catches my eye`（that后置）/ `before I know it I've gone over` · `shopping for groceries always ends up costing more`（动名词主语）/ `sneak into the basket` · `eating out` · `an age where experiences matter more than saving`（where后置）· `splash out on gadgets and clothes` / `looking trendy feels pretty important`（动名词主语）· `Life throws surprises at you` · `having some savings tucked away gives you a real safety net`（动名词主语）· `someone with no buffer`（with后置）/ `borrowing at high interest` · `driven by emotion rather than real need` / `cheer themselves up` · `half the gadgets folk own barely get used`（relative后置）· `making wants feel like needs` · `in a great position to help` · `without it really hurting them` / `sets a good example` · `generosity should come from the heart, not from pressure` · `pay top dollar for whatever they're truly passionate about` · `money well spent, not a waste` · `you can't really put a price on peace of mind`。

---

## P2-老21 · A time you encouraged someone to do something they didn't want to — 鼓励妻子跑首场 10K（Event）

> **Cue**: 是谁 / 鼓励做什么 / 反应如何 / 为何鼓励 · 题型 Event

### ①P2 拆解（英式中文珠子 → 范文英文）

| 句/功能 | 英式中文珠子 | 范文英文 |
|---|---|---|
| [opener] | 那么，我想聊的那个时刻 是 当 我 鼓励 我妻子 [去]报名 她 头一场 正经的 跑步 比赛，一个 10公里。 | So, the time I'd like to talk about is when I encouraged my wife to sign up for her very first proper running race, a 10K. |
| [背景] | 她 真的 很迷 健身、一直 去 健身房，所以 她 显然 够 健壮。 | She's really into fitness and hits the gym all the time, so she was clearly fit enough. |
| [反应] | 但 她 一直 拖着 它，而 内心深处 她 紧张。 | But she kept putting it off, and deep down she was nervous. |
| [反应·细] | 她 会 说 类似 的话，"要是 我 完成 不了、或 得 最后一名 呢？" | She'd say things like, "What if I can't finish, or come last?" |
| [反应·细2] | 基本上，她 在 低估 她自己。 | Basically, she was selling herself short. |
| [how] | 所以 我 温和地 一直 坚持。 | So I gently kept at it. |
| [how·细] | 我 提醒 她 她 训练 得 多 刻苦，还 提出 带 我们的 小男孩 Muye 一起 去 给 她 加油。 | I reminded her how hard she'd trained, and offered to bring our little boy Muye along to cheer her on. |
| [结果] | 她 一开始 犹豫，但 最后 她 报了名、跑 完 了 全程、还 事后 绝对 欣喜若狂。 | She was hesitant at first, but in the end she signed up, ran the whole thing, and was absolutely over the moon afterwards. |
| [why/EL] | 我 推 她 的原因 是 我 看得出 她 真的 想 它、却 被 自我怀疑 拖住，而 我 不 想 她 因为 紧张 错过。 | The reason I pushed her is that I could tell she really wanted it but was held back by self-doubt, and I didn't want her to miss out over nerves. |
| [EL·细] | 我是说，我 自己 从没 跑过 10公里！ | I mean, I'd never run 10K myself! |
| [closer] | 所以，对，差不多 就是 这样。 | So yeah, that's pretty much it. |

### ②P2 整段（shadow）

> So, the time I'd like to talk about is when I encouraged my wife to sign up for her very first proper running race, a 10K. She's really into fitness and hits the gym all the time, so she was clearly fit enough. But she kept putting it off, and deep down she was nervous. She'd say things like, "What if I can't finish, or come last?" Basically, she was selling herself short. So I gently kept at it. I reminded her how hard she'd trained, and offered to bring our little boy Muye along to cheer her on. She was hesitant at first, but in the end she signed up, ran the whole thing, and was absolutely over the moon afterwards. The reason I pushed her is that I could tell she really wanted it but was held back by self-doubt, and I didn't want her to miss out over nerves. I mean, I'd never run 10K myself! So yeah, that's pretty much it.

### 句型/模板（可搬走）

- **time opener + when + 动作**：我想聊的时刻是当我鼓励妻子报名首场10K → `the time I'd like to talk about is when I encouraged my wife to sign up for … a 10K`
- **拖 + 内心深处**：她一直拖着它，内心深处她紧张 → `she kept putting it off, and deep down she was nervous`
- **低估自己**：她在低估她自己 → `she was selling herself short`
- **温和坚持 + embedded**：我温和地一直坚持；提醒她训练得多刻苦 → `I gently kept at it` / `reminded her how hard she'd trained`
- **欣喜若狂**：事后绝对欣喜若狂 → `absolutely over the moon afterwards`
- **The reason I pushed her is that … held back by**：我推她的原因是她想却被自我怀疑拖住 → `The reason I pushed her is that … but was held back by self-doubt` / `miss out over nerves`

---

### ①P3 拆解 + ②整段

**Q1. How can leaders encourage their employees?**

| 句 | 英式中文珠子 | 范文英文 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | 嗯，我会说 那 最好的 领导 以身作则、还 真的 大声 表扬 好 工作。 | Well, I'd say the best leaders lead by example and actually praise good work out loud. |
| 2 | 人们 感到 被 重视、当 他们的 努力 被 注意到，所以 他们 更 卖力 推。 | People feel valued when their effort gets noticed, so they push harder. |
| 3 | 朝 员工 扔 钱 有帮助，但 说实话 认可 日常 更 要紧。 | Throwing money at staff helps, but honestly recognition matters more day to day. |
| 4 | 一句 简单的 "干得好" 能 提亮 某人 整个 一周，说句公道话。 | A simple "great job" can lift someone's whole week, to be fair. |

> Well, I'd say the best leaders lead by example and actually praise good work out loud. People feel valued when their effort gets noticed, so they push harder. Throwing money at staff helps, but honestly recognition matters more day to day. A simple "great job" can lift someone's whole week, to be fair.

**Q2. When should parents encourage their children?**

| 句 | 英式中文珠子 | 范文英文 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | 我觉得 父母 应该 介入、大多 当 一个孩子 就要 太早 放弃。 | I reckon parents should step in mostly when a kid's about to give up too soon. |
| 2 | 那 就是 当 一个 小 推动 真的 算，因为 孩子 常常 怀疑 自己、在 他们 甚至 试过 之前。 | That's when a little push really counts, because children often doubt themselves before they've even tried. |
| 3 | 比如，如果 一个孩子 一节 艰难的 课 后 退出 钢琴，温和的 鼓励 让 他们 继续。 | For instance, if a child quits piano after one tough lesson, gentle encouragement keeps them going. |
| 4 | 但 你 不能 强迫 它，显然。 | But you can't force it, obviously. |

> I reckon parents should step in mostly when a kid's about to give up too soon. That's when a little push really counts, because children often doubt themselves before they've even tried. For instance, if a child quits piano after one tough lesson, gentle encouragement keeps them going. But you can't force it, obviously.

**Q3. What kind of encouragement should parents give?**

| 句 | 英式中文珠子 | 范文英文 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | 说实话，我会说 那 最好的 种类 表扬 努力、而不是 结果。 | Honestly, I'd say the best kind praises effort rather than results. |
| 2 | 如果 你 只 在 一个孩子 赢 时 欢呼，他们 会 害怕 失败，所以 他们 会 停止 冒险。 | If you only cheer when a kid wins, they'll dread failing, so they'll stop taking risks. |
| 3 | 但 如果 你 说 "我 骄傲 你 试了"，他们 学到 挣扎 没关系。 | But if you say "I'm proud you tried", they learn it's fine to struggle. |
| 4 | 那 建立 真正的 自信，（那 持续 得 远远 比 空洞的 奉承 久）。 | That builds real confidence, which lasts way longer than empty flattery does. |

> Honestly, I'd say the best kind praises effort rather than results. If you only cheer when a kid wins, they'll dread failing, so they'll stop taking risks. But if you say "I'm proud you tried", they learn it's fine to struggle. That builds real confidence, which lasts way longer than empty flattery does.

**Q4. Do you think some people are better than others at persuading?**

| 句 | 英式中文珠子 | 范文英文 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | 绝对。 | Definitely. |
| 2 | 有些人 就是 天生 有说服力，因为 他们 把 人 读得 好、还 知道 该 按 哪个 按钮。 | Some folk are just naturally persuasive because they read people well and know which buttons to press. |
| 3 | 他们 保持 冷静、先 听、然后 用 一个 方式 框定 事情、（那种 和 你 对上电的）。 | They stay calm, listen first, then frame things in a way that clicks with you. |
| 4 | 说句公道话，它 很多 也 是 练习——销售员 多年 变得 更 犀利。但 天生的 魅力 肯定 给 有些人 一个 领先。 | To be fair, a lot of it's practice too — salespeople get sharper over years. But raw charm certainly gives some people a head start. |

> Definitely. Some folk are just naturally persuasive because they read people well and know which buttons to press. They stay calm, listen first, then frame things in a way that clicks with you. To be fair, a lot of it's practice too — salespeople get sharper over years. But raw charm certainly gives some people a head start.

**Q5. Should children do everything their parents ask them to do?**

| 句 | 英式中文珠子 | 范文英文 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | 不 总是，不。 | Not always, no. |
| 2 | 我觉得 孩子 该 听 大多数 合理的 要求，因为 父母 通常 最 懂。 | I reckon kids should listen to most reasonable requests, since parents usually know best. |
| 3 | 但 盲目的 服从 不 健康，因为 孩子 需要 学会 质疑、还 为 自己 想。 | But blind obedience isn't healthy, because children need to learn to question and think for themselves. |
| 4 | 比如，如果 一个 父母 明显 错了，一个孩子 说出来 其实 是 一个 好 信号。它 关于 平衡，真的。 | For instance, if a parent's clearly wrong, a kid speaking up is actually a good sign. It's about balance, really. |

> Not always, no. I reckon kids should listen to most reasonable requests, since parents usually know best. But blind obedience isn't healthy, because children need to learn to question and think for themselves. For instance, if a parent's clearly wrong, a kid speaking up is actually a good sign. It's about balance, really.

**Q6. How can employers encourage their staff?**

| 句 | 英式中文珠子 | 范文英文 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | 嗯，除了 一份 像样的 薪水，我会说 雇主 该 提供 真正的 机会 [去]成长。 | Well, beyond a decent salary, I'd say employers should offer real chances to grow. |
| 2 | 人们 保持 有动力、当 他们 在 学习 和 往上 走，所以 培训 和 清晰的 目标 帮 很大。 | People stay motivated when they're learning and moving up, so training and clear goals help loads. |
| 3 | 灵活的 工时 这些天 也 要紧。 | Flexible hours matter too these days. |
| 4 | 但 说实话，只是 被 当作 一个 人 对待、而不是 一个 数字 让 大多数 员工 开心。 | But honestly, just being treated like a human rather than a number keeps most staff happy. |

> Well, beyond a decent salary, I'd say employers should offer real chances to grow. People stay motivated when they're learning and moving up, so training and clear goals help loads. Flexible hours matter too these days. But honestly, just being treated like a human rather than a number keeps most staff happy.

**P3 句型/模板**：`lead by example` / `praise good work out loud` · `their effort gets noticed` · `Throwing money at staff helps`（动名词主语）/ `recognition matters more day to day` · `lift someone's whole week` · `step in mostly when a kid's about to give up too soon` · `That's when a little push really counts` · `gentle encouragement keeps them going` · `praises effort rather than results` · `they'll dread failing` / `stop taking risks` · `it's fine to struggle` · `which lasts way longer than empty flattery does`（which后置）· `read people well` / `know which buttons to press`（embedded）· `frame things in a way that clicks with you`（that后置）· `salespeople get sharper over years` / `a head start` · `blind obedience isn't healthy` / `think for themselves` · `a kid speaking up is actually a good sign`（动名词主语）· `real chances to grow` / `learning and moving up` · `just being treated like a human rather than a number keeps most staff happy`（动名词主语）。

## P2-老22 · A short-term job you'd like in a foreign country — 日本小书店（Object/hypo）

> **Cue**: 在哪 / 怎么知道 / 是什么工作 / 为何想做 · 题型 Object/hypo

### ①P2 拆解（英式中文珠子 → 范文英文）

| 句/功能 | 英式中文珠子 | 范文英文 |
|---|---|---|
| [opener] | 那么，我想聊的那份 工作 是 帮着 经营 一家 小小的 独立 书店、在 日本、几个月。 | So, the job I'd like to talk about is helping run a tiny independent bookshop in Japan for a few months. |
| [how know] | 我 去过 京都 几次，而 每一趟 我 一直 注意到 这些 迷人的 小店、（藏 在 后街 里的）。 | I've been to Kyoto a few times, and on every trip I kept noticing these charming little shops tucked away down the backstreets. |
| [how·细] | 我 就 会 幻想 在 它们 之一 的 柜台 后面 工作。 | I'd just daydream about working behind the counter in one of them. |
| [what job] | 那 工作 本身 会 挺 简单。 | The work itself would be pretty simple. |
| [what·细] | 我 会 上 书架、推荐 书名、还 跟 顾客 聊天。 | I'd be shelving books, recommending titles, and chatting with customers. |
| [why] | 它 正合 我 胃口 的原因 是 我 是 一个 巨大的 书 爱好者，尤其 科幻，所以 我 会 如鱼得水。 | The reason it's right up my street is that I'm a massive book lover, sci-fi especially, so I'd be in my element. |
| [why·细] | 此外，我 会 得以 像 一个 当地人 那样 生活、而不是 一个 游客，吸收 那 文化，还 捡 起 一点 日语。 | Plus, I'd get to live like a local rather than a tourist, soak up the culture, and pick up a bit of Japanese. |
| [EL] | 最 吸引 我的 是 那 节奏的 改变。 | What appeals to me most is the change of pace. |
| [EL·细] | 我是说，它 会 是 一个 完全 的 中断、从 我 日常的 例行。 | I mean, it'd be a complete break from my everyday routine. |
| [closer] | 我 知道 它 有点 是 一个 白日梦，但 如果 我 有天 有 机会，我 会 立刻 抓住 它。所以，对，差不多 就是 这样。 | I know it's a bit of a pipe dream, but if I ever got the chance, I'd jump at it. So yeah, that's pretty much it. |

### ②P2 整段（shadow）

> So, the job I'd like to talk about is helping run a tiny independent bookshop in Japan for a few months. I've been to Kyoto a few times, and on every trip I kept noticing these charming little shops tucked away down the backstreets. I'd just daydream about working behind the counter in one of them. The work itself would be pretty simple. I'd be shelving books, recommending titles, and chatting with customers. The reason it's right up my street is that I'm a massive book lover, sci-fi especially, so I'd be in my element. Plus, I'd get to live like a local rather than a tourist, soak up the culture, and pick up a bit of Japanese. What appeals to me most is the change of pace. I mean, it'd be a complete break from my everyday routine. I know it's a bit of a pipe dream, but if I ever got the chance, I'd jump at it. So yeah, that's pretty much it.

### 句型/模板（可搬走）

- **job opener（动名词短语）**：帮着经营一家小独立书店 → `helping run a tiny independent bookshop`
- **分词后置**：藏在后街里的迷人小店 → `charming little shops tucked away down the backstreets`
- **daydream about + 动名词**：幻想在柜台后面工作 → `daydream about working behind the counter`
- **三个-ing并列**：上书架、推荐书名、聊天 → `shelving books, recommending titles, and chatting with customers`
- **The reason it's right up my street is that … + in my element**：正合胃口的原因是我是巨大书迷，会如鱼得水
- **三个动词不定式并列**：像当地人生活、吸收文化、捡起点日语 → `live like a local …, soak up the culture, and pick up a bit of Japanese`
- **What appeals to me most is …**（what主语）+ `a bit of a pipe dream` / `I'd jump at it`

---

### ①P3 拆解 + ②整段

**Q1. What short-term jobs do young people do in other countries?**

| 句 | 英式中文珠子 | 范文英文 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | 相当 几个，其实。 | Quite a few, actually. |
| 2 | 一大堆 年轻人 在 国外 教 他们 自己的 语言、在 滑雪 度假村 做 季节性 工作、或 做 酒吧 和 咖啡馆 班。 | A lot of young folk teach their own language abroad, work seasonal jobs at ski resorts, or do bar and café shifts. |
| 3 | 农场 摘 水果 也 极其 受欢迎，因为 它 容易 拿到、还 付 得 不错。 | Fruit-picking on farms is hugely popular too, since it's easy to land and pays alright. |
| 4 | 说实话，这些 零工 让 他们 资助 他们的 旅行、同时 亲身 吸收 一个 新 文化。 | Honestly, these gigs let them fund their travels while soaking up a new culture firsthand. |

> Quite a few, actually. A lot of young folk teach their own language abroad, work seasonal jobs at ski resorts, or do bar and café shifts. Fruit-picking on farms is hugely popular too, since it's easy to land and pays alright. Honestly, these gigs let them fund their travels while soaking up a new culture firsthand.

**Q2. What challenges do young people face when working abroad?**

| 句 | 英式中文珠子 | 范文英文 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | 那 最大的 是 那 语言 障碍，我会说。 | The biggest one's the language barrier, I'd say. |
| 2 | 如果 你 没法 跟 当地人 好好 聊，甚至 点 餐 都 变成 一场 挣扎。 | If you can't chat properly with locals, even ordering food turns into a struggle. |
| 3 | 除此之外，有 思乡，因为 你 被 从 家人 和 老朋友 切断。 | On top of that, there's homesickness, since you're cut off from family and old friends. |
| 4 | 文化 差异 也 能 难住 人——在 家乡 礼貌的东西 可能 在 完全 别的 地方 显得 粗鲁。 | Cultural differences can throw people too — what's polite back home might come across as rude somewhere else entirely. |

> The biggest one's the language barrier, I'd say. If you can't chat properly with locals, even ordering food turns into a struggle. On top of that, there's homesickness, since you're cut off from family and old friends. Cultural differences can throw people too — what's polite back home might come across as rude somewhere else entirely.

**Q3. What are the benefits of working for an international company?**

| 句 | 英式中文珠子 | 范文英文 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | 一大堆 好处，说实话。 | Loads of perks, honestly. |
| 2 | 你 被 暴露于 来自 各行各业 的人，所以 你 学到 不同的 思考 和 工作 方式。 | You're exposed to people from all walks of life, so you pick up different ways of thinking and working. |
| 3 | 它 也 在 简历上 看起来 很棒，因为 雇主 爱 一个 全球化的 心态。 | It also looks brilliant on a CV, since employers love a global mindset. |
| 4 | 此外，常常 有 一个 真正的 机会 [去]旅行 或 被 派 海外，（那 大多数人 觉得 挺 令人兴奋）。 | Plus, there's often a real chance to travel or get posted overseas, which most folk find pretty exciting. |

> Loads of perks, honestly. You're exposed to people from all walks of life, so you pick up different ways of thinking and working. It also looks brilliant on a CV, since employers love a global mindset. Plus, there's often a real chance to travel or get posted overseas, which most folk find pretty exciting.

**Q4. What personal skills are required to work in an international company?**

| 句 | 英式中文珠子 | 范文英文 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | 沟通 技能 排 第一，当然。 | Communication skills come top, for sure. |
| 2 | 你 得 把 你的 观点 清楚地 传 给 人、（那种 不 共享 你 母语的）。 | You've got to get your point across clearly to people who don't share your first language. |
| 3 | 有 适应性 也 很 要紧，因为 工作 风格 跨 文化 变化 剧烈。 | Being adaptable matters loads too, since work styles vary wildly across cultures. |
| 4 | 我 也 会 加上 耐心 和 一个 开放的 心态——你 不能 只 期待 每个人 完全 按 你的 方式 做事。 | I'd also throw in patience and an open mind — you can't just expect everyone to do things exactly your way. |

> Communication skills come top, for sure. You've got to get your point across clearly to people who don't share your first language. Being adaptable matters loads too, since work styles vary wildly across cultures. I'd also throw in patience and an open mind — you can't just expect everyone to do things exactly your way.

**Q5. What kind of work can young people do in foreign countries?**

| 句 | 英式中文珠子 | 范文英文 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | 大多是 招待业 工作，我觉得——端 盘子、调 酒、或 在 旅舍 当 人手。 | Mostly hospitality work, I reckon — waiting tables, bartending, or staffing hostels. |
| 2 | 辅导 他们的 母语 是 另一个 大的，尤其 教 英语。 | Tutoring their native tongue is another big one, especially teaching English. |
| 3 | 有些 去 做 亲手的 活，比如 农场 劳作 或 为 家庭 当 互惠生。 | Some go for hands-on stuff like farm labour or au pairing for families. |
| 4 | 这些 工作 通常 不 要求 花哨的 资历，所以 它们 对 某个 年轻人、（那种 只是 热衷 [去]在 国外 住 一阵的）现实。 | These jobs don't usually demand fancy qualifications, so they're realistic for someone young who's just keen to live abroad for a bit. |

> Mostly hospitality work, I reckon — waiting tables, bartending, or staffing hostels. Tutoring their native tongue is another big one, especially teaching English. Some go for hands-on stuff like farm labour or au pairing for families. These jobs don't usually demand fancy qualifications, so they're realistic for someone young who's just keen to live abroad for a bit.

**Q6. Why are some people unwilling to work in other countries?**

| 句 | 英式中文珠子 | 范文英文 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | 相当 一些 被 离开 他们的 舒适区 吓到，我会说。 | A fair few feel daunted by leaving their comfort zone, I'd say. |
| 2 | 他们 宁愿 待 在 家人 和 朋友 附近、也不 在 某个 陌生的 地方 从零 开始。 | They'd rather stay near family and friends than start from scratch somewhere strange. |
| 3 | 那 语言 障碍 也 让 人 却步，那 签证 和 文书 的 麻烦 也 一样。 | The language hurdle puts people off too, and so does the hassle of visas and paperwork. |
| 4 | 说句公道话，有些 只是 安定了、还 满意 他们 已经 建立的 生活。 | To be fair, some are simply settled and happy with the life they've already built. |

> A fair few feel daunted by leaving their comfort zone, I'd say. They'd rather stay near family and friends than start from scratch somewhere strange. The language hurdle puts people off too, and so does the hassle of visas and paperwork. To be fair, some are simply settled and happy with the life they've already built.

**P3 句型/模板**：`work seasonal jobs at ski resorts` · `Fruit-picking on farms is hugely popular`（动名词主语）/ `easy to land` · `fund their travels while soaking up a new culture firsthand` · `even ordering food turns into a struggle`（动名词主语）· `there's homesickness` / `cut off from family and old friends` · `what's polite back home might come across as rude somewhere else`（what主语）· `people from all walks of life` · `looks brilliant on a CV` / `a global mindset` · `get posted overseas, which most folk find pretty exciting`（which后置）· `get your point across clearly to people who don't share your first language`（who后置）· `Being adaptable matters loads`（动名词主语）/ `vary wildly across cultures` · `throw in patience and an open mind` · `waiting tables, bartending, or staffing hostels` · `Tutoring their native tongue is another big one`（动名词主语）· `hands-on stuff like farm labour or au pairing` · `someone young who's just keen to live abroad for a bit`（who后置）· `feel daunted by leaving their comfort zone` · `start from scratch somewhere strange` · `The language hurdle puts people off … and so does the hassle of visas`（倒装）· `the life they've already built`（relative后置）。

## P2-老23 · A person who likes to look after the natural world — 环保志愿者好友（Person）

> **Cue**: 是谁 / 做什么 / 怎么做 / 多常做 / 你的感受 · 题型 Person

### ①P2 拆解（英式中文珠子 → 范文英文）

| 句/功能 | 英式中文珠子 | 范文英文 |
|---|---|---|
| [opener] | 我想聊的那个人 是 我的 一个 亲密的朋友，一个 人、（那种 真心 热衷于 保护 户外的）。 | The person I'd like to talk about is a close friend of mine, someone who's genuinely passionate about protecting the outdoors. |
| [背景] | 我们 已经 做 哥们儿 好些年了，而 照看 那 环境 基本上 是 他 的 全部。 | We've been mates for years now, and looking after the environment is basically his whole thing. |
| [what] | 他 做的 是 放弃 他的 周末 [去]照看 自然。 | What he does is give up his weekends to look after nature. |
| [what·细] | 他 参加 垃圾 清理、沿 山道 和 河岸，参与 植树 活动，还 悄悄 推 他的 朋友 [去]减少 一次性 塑料。 | He joins litter clean-ups along mountain trails and riverbanks, takes part in tree-planting drives, and quietly nudges his friends to cut down on single-use plastic. |
| [how] | 他 着手 它 的方式 真的 打动 我——他 亲力亲为、低调，从不 说教。 | The way he goes about it really gets me — he's hands-on and low-key, never preachy. |
| [how·细] | 他 就 出现，垃圾袋 在手，还 以身作则。 | He just shows up, bin bag in hand, and leads by example. |
| [how often] | 他 差不多 每隔 一个 周末 在 那儿，加上 一年 几场 有组织的 清理。 | He's out there pretty much every other weekend, plus a few organised clean-ups a year. |
| [feel/EL] | 说实话，我 真的 佩服 他的 投入，因为 他 真心 言行一致。 | Honestly, I really admire his dedication, because he genuinely practises what he preaches. |
| [EL·细] | 而 我 说实话，它 蹭 到 我 身上——我 自己 开始 对 废物 更 小心。 | And I'll be honest, it's rubbed off on me — I've started being more careful about waste myself. |
| [closer] | 所以，对，那 就是 那种 朋友、（我 幸运 有的）。 | So yeah, that's the sort of friend I'm lucky to have. |

### ②P2 整段（shadow）

> The person I'd like to talk about is a close friend of mine, someone who's genuinely passionate about protecting the outdoors. We've been mates for years now, and looking after the environment is basically his whole thing. What he does is give up his weekends to look after nature. He joins litter clean-ups along mountain trails and riverbanks, takes part in tree-planting drives, and quietly nudges his friends to cut down on single-use plastic. The way he goes about it really gets me — he's hands-on and low-key, never preachy. He just shows up, bin bag in hand, and leads by example. He's out there pretty much every other weekend, plus a few organised clean-ups a year. Honestly, I really admire his dedication, because he genuinely practises what he preaches. And I'll be honest, it's rubbed off on me — I've started being more careful about waste myself. So yeah, that's the sort of friend I'm lucky to have.

### 句型/模板（可搬走）

- **person opener + who后置**：一个真心热衷保护户外的人 → `someone who's genuinely passionate about protecting the outdoors`
- **动名词主语**：照看环境基本上是他的全部 → `looking after the environment is basically his whole thing`
- **What he does is …（what主语）**：他做的是放弃周末去照看自然 → `What he does is give up his weekends to look after nature`
- **伪分裂主语**：他着手它的方式真的打动我 → `The way he goes about it really gets me`
- **独立主格**：他就出现，垃圾袋在手，以身作则 → `He just shows up, bin bag in hand, and leads by example`
- **what 宾语**：他真心言行一致 → `he genuinely practises what he preaches`
- **收尾 relative后置**：那种我幸运拥有的朋友 → `the sort of friend I'm lucky to have`

---

### ①P3 拆解 + ②整段

**Q1. Do you think parents should teach their children how to protect the environment?**

| 句 | 英式中文珠子 | 范文英文 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | 绝对。 | Absolutely. |
| 2 | 我觉得 习惯、（在家 养成的）倾向于 陪伴 一辈子，所以 如果 父母 回收、省 水，孩子 就 自然 抄 他们。 | I reckon habits formed at home tend to stick for life, so if parents recycle and save water, kids just copy them naturally. |
| 3 | 比如，一个孩子、（那种 被 教 关 灯的）大概 成年后 会 继续 做 它。 | For instance, a child who's taught to switch off lights will probably keep doing it as an adult. |
| 4 | 此外，学校 单独 很难 消除 坏 习惯、（在家 捡来的）。 | Plus, it's hard for schools alone to undo bad habits picked up at home. |

> Absolutely. I reckon habits formed at home tend to stick for life, so if parents recycle and save water, kids just copy them naturally. For instance, a child who's taught to switch off lights will probably keep doing it as an adult. Plus, it's hard for schools alone to undo bad habits picked up at home.

**Q2. What laws about the environment are effective in your country?**

| 句 | 英式中文珠子 | 范文英文 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | 说实话，我会说 那 塑料袋 收费 最 有效——一旦 人们 得 付钱，他们 突然 记得 带 他们 自己的 袋子。 | Honestly, I'd say the plastic-bag charge works best — once people have to pay, they suddenly remember to bring their own bags. |
| 2 | 大城市 的 回收 规则 也 有帮助，因为 分类 你的 垃圾 变成 第二天性。 | Recycling rules in big cities help too, since sorting your rubbish becomes second nature. |
| 3 | 但 说句公道话，有些 法律 纸面上 好看、却 没 被 好好 执行，所以 它们 并不 真的 改变 行为。 | But to be fair, some laws look good on paper yet aren't enforced properly, so they don't really change behaviour. |

> Honestly, I'd say the plastic-bag charge works best — once people have to pay, they suddenly remember to bring their own bags. Recycling rules in big cities help too, since sorting your rubbish becomes second nature. But to be fair, some laws look good on paper yet aren't enforced properly, so they don't really change behaviour.

**Q3. Which do you think people prefer, rewards or punishment, when it comes to government intervention in environmental protection?**

| 句 | 英式中文珠子 | 范文英文 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | 我会说 大多数 人 对 奖励 反应 更好，说实话。 | I'd say most people respond better to rewards, honestly. |
| 2 | 当 你 提供 现金 返还、为 退 瓶子 或 更便宜的 账单、为 用 更少 电，它 感觉 鼓励 而不是 威胁。 | When you offer cash back for returning bottles or cheaper bills for using less power, it feels encouraging rather than threatening. |
| 3 | 惩罚 能 滋生 怨恨，而 人们 找 漏洞。 | Punishment can breed resentment, and folk look for loopholes. |
| 4 | 话虽如此，一个 混合 最 有效——奖励 [去]激励，用 罚款 作为 一个 后备、为 严重的 违规者。 | Having said that, a mix works best — rewards to motivate, with fines as a backup for serious offenders. |

> I'd say most people respond better to rewards, honestly. When you offer cash back for returning bottles or cheaper bills for using less power, it feels encouraging rather than threatening. Punishment can breed resentment, and folk look for loopholes. Having said that, a mix works best — rewards to motivate, with fines as a backup for serious offenders.

**Q4. Is it easy for children in cities to get close to the natural world?**

| 句 | 英式中文珠子 | 范文英文 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | 不太，说实话。 | Not really, to be honest. |
| 2 | 城市 孩子 被 混凝土 包围，所以 除非 附近 有 一个 像样的 公园，他们 很少 日常 看到 正经的 绿意。 | City kids are surrounded by concrete, so unless there's a decent park nearby, they rarely see proper greenery day to day. |
| 3 | 他们的 父母 忙，而 周末 常常 被 各种 课 吃掉。 | Their parents are busy, and weekends often get eaten up by classes. |
| 4 | 话虽如此，一趟 快速的 乡下 或 植物园 之行 仍然 能 给 他们 一个 真正的 自然 味道。 | Having said that, a quick trip to the countryside or a botanical garden can still give them a real taste of nature. |

> Not really, to be honest. City kids are surrounded by concrete, so unless there's a decent park nearby, they rarely see proper greenery day to day. Their parents are busy, and weekends often get eaten up by classes. Having said that, a quick trip to the countryside or a botanical garden can still give them a real taste of nature.

**Q5. What can people do to protect the natural world?**

| 句 | 英式中文珠子 | 范文英文 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | 一大堆 小 步骤，真的。 | Loads of small steps, really. |
| 2 | 人们 能 减少 一次性 塑料、坐 公共交通 而不是 开车、还 在家 好好 回收。 | People can cut down on single-use plastic, take public transport instead of driving, and recycle properly at home. |
| 3 | 参加 本地 清理 也 有用，因为 海滩 和 步道上 的 垃圾 真的 伤害 野生动物。 | Joining local clean-ups makes a difference too, since rubbish on beaches and trails really harms wildlife. |
| 4 | 说句公道话，没 一样 感觉 英勇，但 那些 日常 选择 加起来、跨 整个 社区。 | To be fair, none of it feels heroic, but those everyday choices add up across a whole community. |

> Loads of small steps, really. People can cut down on single-use plastic, take public transport instead of driving, and recycle properly at home. Joining local clean-ups makes a difference too, since rubbish on beaches and trails really harms wildlife. To be fair, none of it feels heroic, but those everyday choices add up across a whole community.

**Q6. Is it important to teach students environmental protection at school?**

| 句 | 英式中文珠子 | 范文英文 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | 绝对。 | Definitely. |
| 2 | 我觉得 学校 塑造 孩子 怎么 看 那世界，所以 早早 织入 环境 话题 种下 那 正确的 价值观。 | I reckon schools shape how kids see the world, so weaving in environmental topics early plants the right values. |
| 3 | 比如，一个 简单的 关于 回收 的 课堂 项目 能 激起 真正的 好奇心、关于 废物。 | For example, a simple class project on recycling can spark genuine curiosity about waste. |
| 4 | 此外，孩子 事后 常常 唠叨 他们的 父母，所以 那 信息 也 传 回 家。它 是 最便宜的 方式之一、[去]建立 持久的 意识。 | Plus, children often nag their parents afterwards, so the message spreads home too. It's one of the cheapest ways to build lasting awareness. |

> Definitely. I reckon schools shape how kids see the world, so weaving in environmental topics early plants the right values. For example, a simple class project on recycling can spark genuine curiosity about waste. Plus, children often nag their parents afterwards, so the message spreads home too. It's one of the cheapest ways to build lasting awareness.

**P3 句型/模板**：`habits formed at home tend to stick for life`（分词后置）· `a child who's taught to switch off lights`（who后置）· `bad habits picked up at home`（分词后置）· `the plastic-bag charge works best` · `sorting your rubbish becomes second nature`（动名词主语）· `some laws look good on paper yet aren't enforced properly` · `respond better to rewards` · `cash back for returning bottles` / `encouraging rather than threatening` · `breed resentment` / `look for loopholes` · `with fines as a backup for serious offenders` · `unless there's a decent park nearby` / `proper greenery day to day` · `weekends often get eaten up by classes` · `a real taste of nature` · `Joining local clean-ups makes a difference`（动名词主语）· `those everyday choices add up` · `schools shape how kids see the world`（embedded）/ `weaving in environmental topics early plants the right values`（动名词主语）· `spark genuine curiosity about waste` · `build lasting awareness`。

## P2-老24 · A shop you enjoy visiting — 成都 MUJI（Place）

> **Cue**: 店名 / 在哪 / 多常去 / 为何喜欢 · 题型 Place

### ①P2 拆解（英式中文珠子 → 范文英文）

| 句/功能 | 英式中文珠子 | 范文英文 |
|---|---|---|
| [opener] | 那么，我想聊的那家 商店 是 MUJI，那个 日本 生活方式 品牌、（那种 卖 简单的 家居用品 和 文具的）。 | So, the shop I'd like to talk about is MUJI, the Japanese lifestyle brand that sells simple homeware and stationery. |
| [where/how often] | 它 藏 在 大 购物中心 之一 里面、就 在 成都 这儿、（我 住的地方），而 我 一个月 一两次 顺道 去，通常 为 一次 放松的 周末 逛、跟 我妻子。 | It's tucked inside one of the big shopping malls here in Chengdu where I live, and I drop by once or twice a month, usually for a relaxed weekend browse with my wife. |
| [why] | 我 最爱的 是 它 那 平静的、不杂乱的 感觉。 | What I love most is the calm, uncluttered feel of it. |
| [why·细] | 我是说，它 是 一个 这么 好的 逃离、从 一个 繁忙商场 的 喧闹。 | I mean, it's such a nice escape from the noise of a busy mall. |
| [why·细2] | 那些 产品 真的 简单、还 顺眼，而 我 能 愉快地 浏览 那 文具 和 储物盒 好久。 | The products are really simple and easy on the eye, and I could happily browse the stationery and storage boxes for ages. |
| [why·细3] | 此外，那 员工 一点 都 不 咄咄逼人，所以 我 能 慢慢来、四处 逛。 | Plus, the staff aren't pushy at all, so I can take my time and wander around. |
| [细] | 十有八九，我 走出来时 带着 一个 小 笔记本 或 一些 盒子、（我 并没 真的 打算 买的）。 | More often than not, I walk out with a little notebook or some boxes I didn't really plan on buying. |
| [EL] | 但 说实话，对我 它 更少 关于 购物、更多 关于 放松。 | But honestly, for me it's less about shopping and more about unwinding. |
| [closer] | 所以，对，它 基本上 是 我 的 方式、[去]在 一个 忙乱的 周末 按 下 暂停。 | So yeah, it's basically my way of pressing pause on a hectic weekend. |

### ②P2 整段（shadow）

> So, the shop I'd like to talk about is MUJI, the Japanese lifestyle brand that sells simple homeware and stationery. It's tucked inside one of the big shopping malls here in Chengdu where I live, and I drop by once or twice a month, usually for a relaxed weekend browse with my wife. What I love most is the calm, uncluttered feel of it. I mean, it's such a nice escape from the noise of a busy mall. The products are really simple and easy on the eye, and I could happily browse the stationery and storage boxes for ages. Plus, the staff aren't pushy at all, so I can take my time and wander around. More often than not, I walk out with a little notebook or some boxes I didn't really plan on buying. But honestly, for me it's less about shopping and more about unwinding. So yeah, it's basically my way of pressing pause on a hectic weekend.

### 句型/模板（可搬走）

- **shop opener + that后置**：MUJI，那个卖简单家居文具的日本生活方式品牌 → `MUJI, the Japanese lifestyle brand that sells simple homeware and stationery`
- **tucked inside … where I live**：藏在成都我住的地方的大商场里 → `tucked inside one of the big shopping malls … where I live` / `drop by once or twice a month`
- **What I love most is …**（what主语）：我最爱的是它平静不杂乱的感觉
- **such a nice escape from …**：一个这么好的逃离，从繁忙商场的喧闹
- **顺眼/逛好久**：产品简单顺眼，我能愉快浏览好久 → `easy on the eye` / `browse … for ages`
- **More often than not, I walk out with …**：十有八九我走出来带着没打算买的东西
- **less about … and more about …**：更少关于购物、更多关于放松 → `less about shopping and more about unwinding`
- **金句**：按下暂停一个忙乱周末 → `pressing pause on a hectic weekend`

---

### ①P3 拆解 + ②整段

**Q1. Do people in your country go to the shopping mall frequently?**

| 句 | 英式中文珠子 | 范文英文 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | 是的，相当 多，说实话。 | Yeah, quite a lot, honestly. |
| 2 | 我会说 商场 已经 变成 更 是 一个 社交 中心、而不 只是 一个 购物 的地方。 | I'd say malls have become more of a social hub than just a place to shop. |
| 3 | 人们 去 那儿 [为了] 抓 一顿饭、看 一场 电影、或 就 跟 朋友 四处 溜达。 | People head there to grab a meal, catch a film, or just stroll around with friends. |
| 4 | 因为 天气 可能 糟糕，它 是 一个 顺手的 地点、（那种 全 在 一个 屋顶 下的），所以 家庭 最后 几乎 每个 周末 去。 | Since the weather can be rough, it's a handy spot that's all under one roof, so families end up going almost every weekend. |

> Yeah, quite a lot, honestly. I'd say malls have become more of a social hub than just a place to shop. People head there to grab a meal, catch a film, or just stroll around with friends. Since the weather can be rough, it's a handy spot that's all under one roof, so families end up going almost every weekend.

**Q2. How have people's shopping habits changed in recent decades?**

| 句 | 英式中文珠子 | 范文英文 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | 巨大地，我觉得。 | Massively, I reckon. |
| 2 | 那 最大的 转变 是 网购——人们 现在 在 手机上 买 几乎 一切，从 杂货 到 衣服。 | The biggest shift's online shopping — people now buy almost everything on their phones, from groceries to clothes. |
| 3 | 他们 也 比 以前 更 在乎 评价 和 快 配送。 | They also care more about reviews and quick delivery than before. |
| 4 | 话虽如此，人们 仍然 享受 一趟 实体 之行、为 更大的 购买，因为 你 真的 没法 单从 一个 屏幕 判断 质量。所以 现在 它 是 一个 混合。 | Having said that, folk still enjoy a physical trip for bigger buys, since you can't really judge quality from a screen alone. So it's a mix now. |

> Massively, I reckon. The biggest shift's online shopping — people now buy almost everything on their phones, from groceries to clothes. They also care more about reviews and quick delivery than before. Having said that, folk still enjoy a physical trip for bigger buys, since you can't really judge quality from a screen alone. So it's a mix now.

**Q3. Do you think shops and shopping malls will disappear in the future?**

| 句 | 英式中文珠子 | 范文英文 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | 我 怀疑 它们 会 完全 消失。 | I doubt they'll vanish completely. |
| 2 | 当然，网购 一直 增长，但 商场 提供 某种东西、（一个 屏幕 做不到的）——那 机会 [去]摸 产品、跟 哥们儿 一起 混。 | Sure, online shopping keeps growing, but malls offer something a screen can't — the chance to touch products and hang out with mates. |
| 3 | 我会说 它们 会 就 演变 成 更 是 一个 体验，带 餐厅 和 娱乐。 | I'd say they'll just evolve into more of an experience, with restaurants and entertainment. |
| 4 | 所以 与其 消失，它们 大概 会 重塑 自己 [去]保持 相关，（那 对我 说得通）。 | So instead of disappearing, they'll probably reinvent themselves to stay relevant, which makes sense to me. |

> I doubt they'll vanish completely. Sure, online shopping keeps growing, but malls offer something a screen can't — the chance to touch products and hang out with mates. I'd say they'll just evolve into more of an experience, with restaurants and entertainment. So instead of disappearing, they'll probably reinvent themselves to stay relevant, which makes sense to me.

**Q4. What are the differences between shopping in street markets and big shopping malls?**

| 句 | 英式中文珠子 | 范文英文 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | 相当 几个，其实。 | Quite a few, actually. |
| 2 | 街头 市场 感觉 更 有生气、而 你 能 讨价还价，加上 价格 倾向于 更低，但 那 质量 有点 碰运气。 | Street markets feel livelier and you can haggle, plus prices tend to be lower, but the quality's a bit hit-or-miss. |
| 3 | 商场，另一方面，更 干净、更 有条理，带 固定 价格 和 空调。 | Malls, on the other hand, are cleaner and better organised, with fixed prices and air conditioning. |
| 4 | 我会说 市场 有 更多 个性，而 商场 赢 在 便捷，所以 它 真的 取决于 你 追求 什么。 | I'd say markets have more character, while malls win on convenience, so it really depends what you're after. |

> Quite a few, actually. Street markets feel livelier and you can haggle, plus prices tend to be lower, but the quality's a bit hit-or-miss. Malls, on the other hand, are cleaner and better organised, with fixed prices and air conditioning. I'd say markets have more character, while malls win on convenience, so it really depends what you're after.

**Q5. What are the differences in the shopping habits of different age groups?**

| 句 | 英式中文珠子 | 范文英文 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | 绝对。 | Definitely. |
| 2 | 年轻人 倾向 网购、还 追 潮流，所以 他们 冲动购买 挺多。 | Younger people lean towards online shopping and chase trends, so they buy on impulse a fair bit. |
| 3 | 老年人，不过，通常 更喜欢 亲自 浏览、还 坚持 品牌、（他们 信任的）。 | Older folk, though, usually prefer browsing in person and sticking to brands they trust. |
| 4 | 他们 也 对 钱 更 小心，我觉得。比如，一个 青少年 可能 每周 订 衣服，而 一个 祖父母 一个月 一次 买 必需品。 | They're also more careful with money, I reckon. For instance, a teenager might order clothes weekly, while a grandparent shops once a month for essentials. |

> Definitely. Younger people lean towards online shopping and chase trends, so they buy on impulse a fair bit. Older folk, though, usually prefer browsing in person and sticking to brands they trust. They're also more careful with money, I reckon. For instance, a teenager might order clothes weekly, while a grandparent shops once a month for essentials.

**Q6. What are the differences between shopping online and in-store?**

| 句 | 英式中文珠子 | 范文英文 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | 有 一个 大 差距，说实话。 | There's a big gap, honestly. |
| 2 | 网上 全 关于 便捷——你 能 比较 价格、还 在 午夜 穿着 你的 睡衣 购物。 | Online's all about convenience — you can compare prices and shop at midnight in your pyjamas. |
| 3 | 店里，不过，你 得以 检查 产品、还 立刻 把 它们 带 回家，不用 等。 | In-store, though, you get to inspect products and take them home straightaway, with no waiting. |
| 4 | 我会说 网上 适合 日常 购买，而 店里 更好、当 你 想 在 花钱 之前 确定，尤其 对 更贵的 物件。 | I'd say online suits routine buys, while in-store's better when you want to be sure before spending, especially on pricier items. |

> There's a big gap, honestly. Online's all about convenience — you can compare prices and shop at midnight in your pyjamas. In-store, though, you get to inspect products and take them home straightaway, with no waiting. I'd say online suits routine buys, while in-store's better when you want to be sure before spending, especially on pricier items.

**P3 句型/模板**：`more of a social hub than just a place to shop` · `grab a meal, catch a film, or just stroll around` · `a handy spot that's all under one roof`（that后置）· `The biggest shift's online shopping` · `care more about reviews and quick delivery` · `judge quality from a screen alone` · `I doubt they'll vanish completely` · `something a screen can't`（relative后置）/ `the chance to touch products and hang out with mates` · `evolve into more of an experience` · `reinvent themselves to stay relevant, which makes sense to me`（which后置）· `you can haggle` / `the quality's a bit hit-or-miss` · `with fixed prices and air conditioning` · `markets have more character` / `what you're after`（embedded）· `buy on impulse a fair bit` · `browsing in person` / `brands they trust`（relative后置）· `more careful with money` · `There's a big gap`（dummy-there）· `shop at midnight in your pyjamas` · `inspect products and take them home straightaway` · `online suits routine buys` / `when you want to be sure before spending`。

## P2-老25 · A city you enjoyed visiting — 重庆（Place）

> **Cue**: 在哪 / 何时去 / 待多久 / 做了什么 / 为何享受 · 题型 Place

### ①P2 拆解（英式中文珠子 → 范文英文）

| 句/功能 | 英式中文珠子 | 范文英文 |
|---|---|---|
| [opener] | 那么，我想聊的那座 城市 是 重庆，一个 巨大的、多山的 地方、在 中国 西南，离 成都 不太远、（我 住的地方）。 | So, the city I'd like to talk about is Chongqing, a huge, hilly place in southwest China, not too far from Chengdu where I live. |
| [when/how long] | 我 几年前 去 那儿、在 一个 长周末，而 我们 待 了 大约 三四天。 | I went there a couple of years ago, over a long weekend, and we stayed for about three or four days. |
| [who] | 它 是 一次 家庭 旅行，所以 我 跟 我妻子 和 我们的 儿子 Muye 一起 去，（那时 他 五岁）。 | It was a family trip, so I went with my wife and our son Muye, who was five at the time. |
| [what] | 我们 在 那儿 做 了 一大堆。 | We did loads there. |
| [what·细] | 我们 吃 那 有名的 火辣 火锅，（那 差点 把 我的 头 掀了），还 坐 那 轻轨 列车、（那种 直接 穿 过 一栋 居民楼的）。 | We ate the famous fiery hotpot, which nearly blew my head off, and rode the light-rail train that slices straight through a residential building. |
| [what·细2] | 除此之外，晚上 我们 看 那 河边 老城 全 点亮，像 某种 出自 一部 动画 的东西。 | On top of that, at night we saw the riverside old town all lit up, like something out of an animation. |
| [why/EL] | 但 我 真正 爱的 是 那 狂野的、垂直的 布局。 | But what I really loved was the wild, vertical layout. |
| [why·细] | 我是说，那些 路 和 建筑 一个 摞 在 另一个 上面，所以 你 失去 所有 感觉、关于 你 在 哪一层。 | I mean, the roads and buildings are stacked on top of each other, so you lose all sense of which floor you're on. |
| [why·细2] | 此外 那 夜景 是 一场 眼睛 的 盛宴。 | Plus the night views were a feast for the eyes. |
| [closer] | 所以，对，它 很 值 这趟。 | So yeah, it was well worth the trip. |

### ②P2 整段（shadow）

> So, the city I'd like to talk about is Chongqing, a huge, hilly place in southwest China, not too far from Chengdu where I live. I went there a couple of years ago, over a long weekend, and we stayed for about three or four days. It was a family trip, so I went with my wife and our son Muye, who was five at the time. We did loads there. We ate the famous fiery hotpot, which nearly blew my head off, and rode the light-rail train that slices straight through a residential building. On top of that, at night we saw the riverside old town all lit up, like something out of an animation. But what I really loved was the wild, vertical layout. I mean, the roads and buildings are stacked on top of each other, so you lose all sense of which floor you're on. Plus the night views were a feast for the eyes. So yeah, it was well worth the trip.

### 句型/模板（可搬走）

- **city opener + 同位 + where后置**：重庆，一个中国西南的多山大城市，离我住的成都不远 → `Chongqing, a huge, hilly place …, not too far from Chengdu where I live`
- **who后置**：我们的儿子Muye，那时他五岁 → `our son Muye, who was five at the time`
- **which/that后置**：差点掀我头的火锅；直接穿过居民楼的轻轨 → `hotpot, which nearly blew my head off` / `the train that slices straight through a residential building`
- **全点亮**：老城全点亮，像出自动画的东西 → `all lit up, like something out of an animation`
- **what 主语**：我真正爱的是那狂野垂直的布局 → `what I really loved was the wild, vertical layout`
- **摞在一起 + embedded**：路和建筑摞在一起，你失去在哪一层的感觉 → `stacked on top of each other, so you lose all sense of which floor you're on`
- **金句**：一场眼睛的盛宴 / 很值这趟 → `a feast for the eyes` / `well worth the trip`

---

### ①P3 拆解 + ②整段

**Q1. What kinds of facilities do big cities have?**

| 句 | 英式中文珠子 | 范文英文 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | 嗯，我会说 大 城市 倾向于 有 几乎 一切、（你 会 需要的）。 | Well, I'd say big cities tend to have pretty much everything you'd need. |
| 2 | 你 有 正经的 医院、好的 公共交通、比如 地铁 和 公交，加上 一大堆 购物中心 和 公园。 | You've got proper hospitals, good public transport like metros and buses, plus loads of shopping malls and parks. |
| 3 | 除此之外，它们 通常 有 大学 和 娱乐 地点、比如 电影院。 | On top of that, they usually have universities and entertainment spots like cinemas. |
| 4 | 基本上，它 全 塞 进 一个 地方，（那 真的 顺手）。 | Basically, it's all packed into one place, which is really handy. |

> Well, I'd say big cities tend to have pretty much everything you'd need. You've got proper hospitals, good public transport like metros and buses, plus loads of shopping malls and parks. On top of that, they usually have universities and entertainment spots like cinemas. Basically, it's all packed into one place, which is really handy.

**Q2. Do you think modern cities are suitable for young people or old people?**

| 句 | 英式中文珠子 | 范文英文 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | 说实话，我觉得 它们 更 适合 年轻人。 | Honestly, I reckon they suit young people more. |
| 2 | 那 节奏 快，而 有 一大堆 工作 机会，（那 年轻人 通常 爱）。 | The pace is fast and there're tons of job chances, which younger folk usually love. |
| 3 | 老年人 可能 觉得 它 太 吵、太 拥挤，而 那 生活 成本 是 一个 真正的 头疼。 | Older people might find it too noisy and crowded, and the cost of living's a real headache. |
| 4 | 话虽如此，城市 确实 提供 更好的 医院，所以 它 不 完全 是 一边倒。 | Having said that, cities do offer better hospitals, so it's not totally one-sided. |

> Honestly, I reckon they suit young people more. The pace is fast and there're tons of job chances, which younger folk usually love. Older people might find it too noisy and crowded, and the cost of living's a real headache. Having said that, cities do offer better hospitals, so it's not totally one-sided.

**Q3. Before you travel to a city, what factors would you consider?**

| 句 | 英式中文珠子 | 范文英文 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | 对我来说，那 第一件事 是 那 成本——住宿 和 食物 能 真的 加起来。 | For me, the first thing's the cost — accommodation and food can really add up. |
| 2 | 然后 我 会 看 那 天气 和 那地方 有 多 安全。 | Then I'd look at the weather and how safe the place is. |
| 3 | 交通 也 要紧，因为 你 不 想 浪费 几小时 卡 在 车流 里。 | Transport matters too, since you don't want to waste hours stuck in traffic. |
| 4 | 此外 我 总是 查 有 什么 可看的，比如 地标 或 值得 一试 的 当地 食物。 | Plus I always check what there's to see, like landmarks or local food worth trying. |

> For me, the first thing's the cost — accommodation and food can really add up. Then I'd look at the weather and how safe the place is. Transport matters too, since you don't want to waste hours stuck in traffic. Plus I always check what there's to see, like landmarks or local food worth trying.

**Q4. What are the disadvantages of living in a very famous city?**

| 句 | 英式中文珠子 | 范文英文 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | 相当 几个，其实。 | Quite a few, actually. |
| 2 | 有名的 城市 被 游客 淹没，所以 它们 拥挤、而 价格 飙升。 | Famous cities get swamped with tourists, so they're crowded and prices shoot up. |
| 3 | 房租 常常 也 高得离谱，（那 让 日常生活 一场 挣扎）。 | Rent's often sky-high too, which makes daily life a struggle. |
| 4 | 此外 那 污染 和 噪音 能 随时间 把 你 磨垮。所以 虽然 住 在 某个 知名的地方 听起来 光鲜，那 现实 常常 有点 累人。 | Plus the pollution and noise can wear you down over time. So while it sounds glamorous to live somewhere well-known, the reality's often a bit exhausting. |

> Quite a few, actually. Famous cities get swamped with tourists, so they're crowded and prices shoot up. Rent's often sky-high too, which makes daily life a struggle. Plus the pollution and noise can wear you down over time. So while it sounds glamorous to live somewhere well-known, the reality's often a bit exhausting.

**Q5. Do you prefer to visit well-developed cities or cities with a long history?**

| 句 | 英式中文珠子 | 范文英文 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | 我 会 倾向 城市、（有 一段 长 历史的），说句公道话。 | I'd lean towards cities with a long history, to be fair. |
| 2 | 有 某种 特别的东西、关于 老 街道 和 古 建筑、（那种 告诉 你 一个 真实的 故事的）。 | There's something special about old streets and ancient buildings that tells you a real story. |
| 3 | 发达的 城市 可能 感觉 有点 千篇一律，到处 是 相同的 商场。 | Well-developed cities can feel a bit samey, with the same malls everywhere. |
| 4 | 但 一个 历史的 地方 有 它 自己的 个性，所以 它 在 你的 记忆里 停留 得 远远 更久。 | But a historic place has its own character, so it sticks in your memory far longer. |

> I'd lean towards cities with a long history, to be fair. There's something special about old streets and ancient buildings that tells you a real story. Well-developed cities can feel a bit samey, with the same malls everywhere. But a historic place has its own character, so it sticks in your memory far longer.

**Q6. For those who live in cities, is it because they want to or have to?**

| 句 | 英式中文珠子 | 范文英文 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | 我会说 它 是 两者 的 混合，真的。 | I'd say it's a mix of both, really. |
| 2 | 有些人 真心 爱 那 热闹 和 那 便捷，所以 他们 开心地 选择 它。 | Some people genuinely love the buzz and the convenience, so they choose it happily. |
| 3 | 但 一大堆 其他人 搬 进来 只 [为了] 追 工作 或 更好的 学校、为 他们的 孩子。 | But plenty of others move in just to chase jobs or better schools for their kids. |
| 4 | 所以 对 他们 它 更 是 一个 不得不、而不是 一个 想要，可惜。 | So for them it's more of a have-to than a want-to, sadly. |

> I'd say it's a mix of both, really. Some people genuinely love the buzz and the convenience, so they choose it happily. But plenty of others move in just to chase jobs or better schools for their kids. So for them it's more of a have-to than a want-to, sadly.

**P3 句型/模板**：`pretty much everything you'd need`（relative后置）· `entertainment spots like cinemas` · `it's all packed into one place, which is really handy`（which后置）· `there're tons of job chances, which younger folk usually love`（which后置）· `the cost of living's a real headache` / `it's not totally one-sided` · `can really add up` · `how safe the place is`（embedded）· `waste hours stuck in traffic` · `what there's to see`（embedded）/ `local food worth trying` · `get swamped with tourists` / `prices shoot up` · `Rent's often sky-high too, which makes daily life a struggle`（which后置）· `wear you down over time` · `cities with a long history`（with后置）· `something special about … that tells you a real story`（that后置）· `feel a bit samey` · `sticks in your memory far longer` · `love the buzz and the convenience` · `more of a have-to than a want-to`。

## P2-老26 · A quiet place you like to go — 成都一处安静小公园（Place）

> **Cue**: 在哪 / 怎么知道 / 多常去 / 做什么 / 你的感受 · 题型 Place

### ①P2 拆解（英式中文珠子 → 范文英文）

| 句/功能 | 英式中文珠子 | 范文英文 |
|---|---|---|
| [opener] | 那么，我想聊的那个 地方 是 一个 小小的、藏起来的 公园、（在 我 成都 家 附近的），带 一个 小 湖 和 一片 竹林。 | So, the place I'd like to talk about is a small, tucked-away park near my home in Chengdu, with a little lake and a bamboo grove. |
| [how knew] | 我 好多年前 偶然 撞见 它，当 我儿子 Muye 还是 一个 婴儿、需要 一些 新鲜 空气。 | I stumbled on it years ago by chance, when my son Muye was a baby and needed some fresh air. |
| [how often] | 它 一点 不 像 那些 有名的 拥挤 公园，所以 我 一直 回去，通常 一周 一两次。 | It's nothing like the famous crowded parks, so I keep going back, usually a couple of times a week. |
| [when] | 我 倾向 一大早 或 黄昏 前后 去，当 它 几乎 空。 | I tend to go early in the morning or around dusk, when it's almost empty. |
| [what] | 大多 我 就 绕着 那 湖 溜达，或 坐 在 一张 长椅上 读 一会儿。 | Mostly I just stroll around the lake, or sit on a bench and read for a while. |
| [what·细] | 其他 时候 我 会 带 Muye 一起 去 喂 鱼，而 我们 会 一起 听 那些 鸟。 | Other times I'll bring Muye along to feed the fish, and we'll listen to the birds together. |
| [细] | 基本上，它 是 我 理清 我 脑子 的地方。 | Basically, it's where I clear my head. |
| [why/EL] | 我 爱 它 的地方 是 它 怎么 让 我 放松。 | What I love about it is how it lets me unwind. |
| [why·细] | 我是说，它 离 那 车流 只 几分钟，但 它 感觉 离 所有 那 喧嚣 几英里 远。 | I mean, it's only minutes from the traffic, but it feels miles away from all the hustle and bustle. |
| [closer] | 所以，对，它 是 我 的 小 逃离，而 我 总是 带着 一种 充电 的 感觉 离开。 | So yeah, it's my little escape, and I always come away feeling recharged. |

### ②P2 整段（shadow）

> So, the place I'd like to talk about is a small, tucked-away park near my home in Chengdu, with a little lake and a bamboo grove. I stumbled on it years ago by chance, when my son Muye was a baby and needed some fresh air. It's nothing like the famous crowded parks, so I keep going back, usually a couple of times a week. I tend to go early in the morning or around dusk, when it's almost empty. Mostly I just stroll around the lake, or sit on a bench and read for a while. Other times I'll bring Muye along to feed the fish, and we'll listen to the birds together. Basically, it's where I clear my head. What I love about it is how it lets me unwind. I mean, it's only minutes from the traffic, but it feels miles away from all the hustle and bustle. So yeah, it's my little escape, and I always come away feeling recharged.

### 句型/模板（可搬走）

- **place opener + with后置**：一个藏起来的小公园，带小湖和竹林 → `a small, tucked-away park … with a little lake and a bamboo grove`
- **偶然撞见 + when**：我好多年前偶然撞见它，当Muye还是婴儿 → `I stumbled on it years ago by chance, when …`
- **一点不像 + 一直回去**：它一点不像有名拥挤公园，所以我一直回去 → `It's nothing like the famous crowded parks, so I keep going back`
- **around dusk, when it's almost empty**：黄昏前后去，当它几乎空
- **where 表语**：它是我理清脑子的地方 → `it's where I clear my head`
- **What 主语 + embedded**：我爱它的地方是它怎么让我放松 → `What I love about it is how it lets me unwind`
- **对比金句**：离车流只几分钟，却感觉离喧嚣几英里远；带着充电感离开 → `only minutes from the traffic, but it feels miles away from all the hustle and bustle` / `come away feeling recharged`

---

### ①P3 拆解 + ②整段

**Q1. Is it easy to find quiet places in your country? Why?**

| 句 | 英式中文珠子 | 范文英文 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | 说实话，它 变得 更 难。 | Honestly, it's getting harder. |
| 2 | 在 像 我们 这样的 大城市，几乎 到处 有 车流 和 人群，所以 真正的 安宁 挺 稀有。 | In big cities like ours, there's traffic and crowds almost everywhere, so true peace and quiet are pretty rare. |
| 3 | 但 如果 你 知道 该 去 哪儿 找，你 仍然 会 找到 小 角落——藏起来的 公园、安静的 寺庙、或 河边的 地点。 | But if you know where to look, you'll still find little pockets — tucked-away parks, quiet temples, or spots by a river. |
| 4 | 它们 只是 不 像 以前 那么 容易 撞见。 | They're just not as easy to stumble on as they used to be. |

> Honestly, it's getting harder. In big cities like ours, there's traffic and crowds almost everywhere, so true peace and quiet are pretty rare. But if you know where to look, you'll still find little pockets — tucked-away parks, quiet temples, or spots by a river. They're just not as easy to stumble on as they used to be.

**Q2. How do people spend their leisure time in your country?**

| 句 | 英式中文珠子 | 范文英文 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | 嗯，它 真的 因人而异。 | Well, it really varies. |
| 2 | 年轻人 倾向 刷 手机、看 剧、或 跟 哥们儿 见面 吃 火锅。 | Younger folk tend to scroll on their phones, watch shows, or meet up with mates for hotpot. |
| 3 | 老年人 偏向 更 平静的 东西，比如 公园里 打 太极 或 打 牌。 | Older people lean towards calmer stuff, like tai chi in the park or playing cards. |
| 4 | 我会说 出去吃 是 一个 大的、跨 所有 年龄，因为 食物 是 一个 巨大 部分、（我们 这儿 怎么 放松 和 社交的）。 | I'd say eating out is a big one across all ages, since food's a huge part of how we relax and socialise here. |

> Well, it really varies. Younger folk tend to scroll on their phones, watch shows, or meet up with mates for hotpot. Older people lean towards calmer stuff, like tai chi in the park or playing cards. I'd say eating out is a big one across all ages, since food's a huge part of how we relax and socialise here.

**Q3. How does technology affect the way people spend their leisure time?**

| 句 | 英式中文珠子 | 范文英文 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | 巨大地，我觉得。 | Massively, I reckon. |
| 2 | 这些天 人们 用 他们的 手机 填 几乎 每一 空闲 分钟——流媒体、打游戏、无休止地 刷。 | These days people fill almost every spare minute with their phones — streaming, gaming, scrolling endlessly. |
| 3 | 它 超 方便，但 它 也 让 我们 有点 更 懒、更 粘 屏幕。 | It's super convenient, but it's also made us a bit lazier and more glued to screens. |
| 4 | 比如，一大堆 人 宁愿 在家 刷 完 一部 剧、也不 出去 散步、像 他们 曾经 做的。 | For instance, plenty of folk would rather binge a series at home than go out for a walk like they once did. |

> Massively, I reckon. These days people fill almost every spare minute with their phones — streaming, gaming, scrolling endlessly. It's super convenient, but it's also made us a bit lazier and more glued to screens. For instance, plenty of folk would rather binge a series at home than go out for a walk like they once did.

**Q4. Do you think only old people have time for leisure?**

| 句 | 英式中文珠子 | 范文英文 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | 一点也不，我会说 那 有点 是 一个 误区。 | Not at all, I'd say that's a bit of a myth. |
| 2 | 当然，退休的人 有 更多 空闲 小时，但 每个人 都 需要 停机时间 [去]充电。 | Sure, retired folk have more free hours, but everyone needs downtime to recharge. |
| 3 | 甚至 忙碌的 工作者 也 挤 出 晚上 或 周末 [去]放松。 | Even busy workers carve out evenings or weekends to unwind. |
| 4 | 说句公道话，年轻人 可能 有 更少的 它，但 他们 仍然 在 任何 他们 能的 地方 挤 进 休闲——它 是 一个 基本的 人类 需求，真的。 | To be fair, younger people might have less of it, but they still squeeze in leisure wherever they can — it's a basic human need, really. |

> Not at all, I'd say that's a bit of a myth. Sure, retired folk have more free hours, but everyone needs downtime to recharge. Even busy workers carve out evenings or weekends to unwind. To be fair, younger people might have less of it, but they still squeeze in leisure wherever they can — it's a basic human need, really.

**Q5. Why do old people prefer to live in quiet places?**

| 句 | 英式中文珠子 | 范文英文 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | 嗯，我 觉得 它 大多 关于 内心的 安宁。 | Well, I think it's mostly about peace of mind. |
| 2 | 在 几十年 的 喧嚣 之后，老年人 渴望 某个 平静的地方、（那里 他们 能 彻底 慢下来）。 | After decades of hustle and bustle, older people crave somewhere calm where they can slow right down. |
| 3 | 安静的 地方 对 他们 也 更 温柔——更少 噪音、更少 压力、更 干净的 空气。 | Quiet places are gentler on them too — less noise, less stress, cleaner air. |
| 4 | 此外，他们 常常 想要 一个 更 安静的 节奏 [去]享受 爱好、比如 园艺 或 就 坐 着 一杯茶。 | Plus, they often want a quieter pace to enjoy hobbies like gardening or simply sitting with a cup of tea. |

> Well, I think it's mostly about peace of mind. After decades of hustle and bustle, older people crave somewhere calm where they can slow right down. Quiet places are gentler on them too — less noise, less stress, cleaner air. Plus, they often want a quieter pace to enjoy hobbies like gardening or simply sitting with a cup of tea.

**Q6. Why are there more noises made at home now than in the past?**

| 句 | 英式中文珠子 | 范文英文 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | 主要 因为 所有 那些 小设备、（我们 现在 拥有的）。 | Mainly because of all the gadgets we own now. |
| 2 | 家里 塞满 电视、音箱、洗衣机、和 孩子、（粘 在 吵闹的 游戏上的）。 | Homes are packed with TVs, speakers, washing machines, and kids glued to noisy games. |
| 3 | 过去，地方 更 简单、更 安静，带 远远 更少的 电器 嗡嗡 响着。 | In the past, places were simpler and quieter, with far fewer appliances humming away. |
| 4 | 此外，现代 公寓 的 墙 常常 更 薄，所以 你 最后 听到 你的 邻居 远远 比 以前 多。 | Plus, walls in modern flats are often thinner, so you end up hearing your neighbours far more than before. |

> Mainly because of all the gadgets we own now. Homes are packed with TVs, speakers, washing machines, and kids glued to noisy games. In the past, places were simpler and quieter, with far fewer appliances humming away. Plus, walls in modern flats are often thinner, so you end up hearing your neighbours far more than before.

**P3 句型/模板**：`it's getting harder` · `there's traffic and crowds almost everywhere` · `if you know where to look`（embedded）/ `little pockets` · `not as easy to stumble on as they used to be` · `meet up with mates for hotpot` · `tai chi in the park or playing cards` · `eating out is a big one`（动名词主语）/ `how we relax and socialise here`（embedded）· `fill almost every spare minute with their phones` · `made us a bit lazier and more glued to screens` · `binge a series at home than go out for a walk like they once did` · `a bit of a myth` · `everyone needs downtime to recharge` · `carve out evenings or weekends to unwind` · `squeeze in leisure wherever they can` · `peace of mind` · `crave somewhere calm where they can slow right down`（where后置）· `gentler on them` · `a quieter pace to enjoy hobbies` · `all the gadgets we own now`（relative后置）· `kids glued to noisy games`（分词后置）· `with far fewer appliances humming away`（with-absolute）· `walls in modern flats are often thinner`。
