## P2-老27 · A TV or online program you like to watch — 烘焙比赛节目（Object）

> **Cue**: 是什么 / 讲什么 / 和谁看 / 为何喜欢 · 题型 Object

### ①P2 拆解（英式中文珠子 → 范文英文）

| 句/功能 | 英式中文珠子 | 范文英文 |
|---|---|---|
| [opener] | 那么，我想聊的那个 节目 是 一个 烘焙 比赛 节目，那种 温馨的、（那里 普通 人 竞争的）。 | So, the programme I'd like to talk about is a baking competition show, the cosy kind where ordinary people compete. |
| [about] | 基本上，它 是 关于 一群 业余的 家庭 烘焙者、在 一个 大帐篷 里，而 每周 他们 做 蛋糕、面包、和 糕点。 | Basically, it's about a group of amateur home bakers in a big tent, and each week they make cakes, breads and pastries. |
| [about·细] | 一个人 每 一轮 离开，所以 有 一场 友好的 竞争 进行着。 | One person leaves every round, so there's a friendly competition going on. |
| [who] | 我 总是 跟 我妻子 一起 看，（她 自己 是 一个 热心的 家庭 烘焙者），而 时不时 我们的 儿子 Muye 加入 那 蛋糕 部分。 | I always watch it with my wife, who's a keen home baker herself, and now and then our son Muye joins in for the cake bits. |
| [why] | 我 爱的 是 它 那么 健康、低风险——没有 刻薄 或 傻 戏剧，只有 可爱的 人 彼此 善良。 | What I love is that it's so wholesome and low-stakes — there's no nastiness or silly drama, just lovely people being kind to each other. |
| [why·细] | 除此之外，我妻子 从 它 得到 一大堆 食谱 灵感。 | On top of that, my wife gets loads of recipe inspiration from it. |
| [why·细2] | 比如，她 其实 试着 在家 重现 几个 那些 烘焙品，（那 非常 好玩）。 | For example, she's actually tried to recreate a few of the bakes at home, which was great fun. |
| [closer] | 到头来，它 是 我们 温馨的 方式、[去]在 一个 周末 晚上 一起 放松——安慰性 观看，基本上，而 我们 总是 为 那些 烘焙者 加油。 | At the end of the day, it's our cosy way to unwind together on a weekend evening — comfort viewing, basically, and we're always rooting for the bakers. |

### ②P2 整段（shadow）

> So, the programme I'd like to talk about is a baking competition show, the cosy kind where ordinary people compete. Basically, it's about a group of amateur home bakers in a big tent, and each week they make cakes, breads and pastries. One person leaves every round, so there's a friendly competition going on. I always watch it with my wife, who's a keen home baker herself, and now and then our son Muye joins in for the cake bits. What I love is that it's so wholesome and low-stakes — there's no nastiness or silly drama, just lovely people being kind to each other. On top of that, my wife gets loads of recipe inspiration from it. For example, she's actually tried to recreate a few of the bakes at home, which was great fun. At the end of the day, it's our cosy way to unwind together on a weekend evening — comfort viewing, basically, and we're always rooting for the bakers.

### 句型/模板（可搬走）

- **programme opener + where后置**：一个温馨的、普通人竞争的烘焙比赛节目 → `a baking competition show, the cosy kind where ordinary people compete`
- **there's … going on**：有一场友好的竞争进行着 → `there's a friendly competition going on`
- **who后置 + 加入**：我妻子，她自己是热心烘焙者；Muye加入蛋糕部分 → `my wife, who's a keen home baker herself` / `joins in for the cake bits`
- **What I love is that …**（what主语）+ 动名词伴随：只有可爱的人彼此善良 → `just lovely people being kind to each other`
- **which后置评论**：她试着在家重现几个，那非常好玩 → `recreate a few of the bakes at home, which was great fun`
- **金句**：安慰性观看 / 为烘焙者加油 → `comfort viewing` / `rooting for the bakers`

---

### ①P3 拆解 + ②整段

**Q1. What programs do people like to watch in your country?**

| 句 | 英式中文珠子 | 范文英文 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | 说实话，真人秀 在这里 巨大——才艺 比赛、烹饪 对战、所有 那种 节目。 | Honestly, reality shows are huge here — talent contests, cooking battles, all that sort of show. |
| 2 | 我觉得 人们 爱 它们 因为 它们 在 漫长的 一天 后 容易 跟上，而 你 不 需要 想 太 用力。 | I reckon people love them because they're easy to follow after a long day, and you don't need to think too hard. |
| 3 | 新闻 和 剧集 也 表现 不错，但 说句公道话，那些 轻松的 倾向于 拉 进 最大的 人群。 | News and drama series do well too, but to be fair, the lighthearted ones tend to pull in the biggest crowds. |

> Honestly, reality shows are huge here — talent contests, cooking battles, all that sort of show. I reckon people love them because they're easy to follow after a long day, and you don't need to think too hard. News and drama series do well too, but to be fair, the lighthearted ones tend to pull in the biggest crowds.

**Q2. Do people in your country like to watch foreign TV programs?**

| 句 | 英式中文珠子 | 范文英文 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | 相当 几个 会，是的，尤其 年轻人。 | Quite a few do, yeah, especially younger folk. |
| 2 | 我会说 流媒体 平台 已经 让 外国 节目 真的 可及，所以 人们 现在 乐意 看 韩国 或 美国 剧、带 字幕。 | I'd say streaming platforms have made foreign shows really accessible, so people happily watch Korean or American series with subtitles now. |
| 3 | 它 给 他们 一个 新鲜的 视角 和 一个 偷看 进 其他 文化。 | It gives them a fresh perspective and a peek into other cultures. |
| 4 | 话虽如此，更 老的 世代 仍然 大多 更喜欢 本地 节目、用 他们 自己的 语言。 | Having said that, older generations still mostly prefer local programmes in their own language. |

> Quite a few do, yeah, especially younger folk. I'd say streaming platforms have made foreign shows really accessible, so people happily watch Korean or American series with subtitles now. It gives them a fresh perspective and a peek into other cultures. Having said that, older generations still mostly prefer local programmes in their own language.

**Q3. What's the benefit of letting kids watch animal videos than visiting zoos?**

| 句 | 英式中文珠子 | 范文英文 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | 嗯，那 主要 好处 是 便捷——你 能 给 孩子 看 一大堆 不同的 动物、来自 世界各地、不 离开 家，（那 一个 动物园 就是 匹配不了的）。 | Well, the main benefit is convenience — you can show kids loads of different animals from around the world without leaving home, which a zoo simply can't match. |
| 2 | 视频 也 捕捉 野生 行为、（你 在 一个 笼子 里 永远 看不到的）。 | Videos also capture wild behaviour you'd never see in a cage. |
| 3 | 话虽如此，没有东西 完全 比得过 那 兴奋、（亲近地 看 一个 真的 动物的），所以 我 会 混 两者。 | Having said that, nothing quite beats the excitement of seeing a real animal up close, so I'd mix both. |

> Well, the main benefit is convenience — you can show kids loads of different animals from around the world without leaving home, which a zoo simply can't match. Videos also capture wild behaviour you'd never see in a cage. Having said that, nothing quite beats the excitement of seeing a real animal up close, so I'd mix both.

**Q4. Do teachers play videos in class in your country?**

| 句 | 英式中文珠子 | 范文英文 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | 是的，其实 挺 常。 | Yeah, quite often actually. |
| 2 | 很多 老师 放 短 片段 [去]打断 一节课、还 视觉地 解释 棘手的 想法，（那 真的 帮 学生 保持 专注）。 | A lot of teachers play short clips to break up a lesson and explain tricky ideas visually, which really helps students stay focused. |
| 3 | 比如，一个 科学 视频 能 展示 一个 实验、（那种 很难 用 词语 描述的）。 | For instance, a science video can show an experiment that's hard to describe in words. |
| 4 | 我 觉得 它 是 一个 聪明的 方式、[去]保持 课堂 活泼、吸引人。 | I think it's a smart way to keep classes lively and engaging. |

> Yeah, quite often actually. A lot of teachers play short clips to break up a lesson and explain tricky ideas visually, which really helps students stay focused. For instance, a science video can show an experiment that's hard to describe in words. I think it's a smart way to keep classes lively and engaging.

**Q5. Do you think watching talk shows is a waste of time?**

| 句 | 英式中文珠子 | 范文英文 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | 不太，不。 | Not really, no. |
| 2 | 我会说 脱口秀 能 真正 有 娱乐性，而 一个 好 主持人 常常 激起 有趣的 辩论、关于 当前 议题。 | I'd say talk shows can be genuinely entertaining, and a good host often sparks interesting debates about current issues. |
| 3 | 所以 你 在 放松、同时 捡起 新 观点。 | So you're relaxing and picking up new viewpoints at the same time. |
| 4 | 说句公道话，有些 是 纯 花架子、带 名人 八卦，但 那些 更好的 说实话 是 一个 好 方式、[去]放松。 | To be fair, some are pure fluff with celebrity gossip, but the better ones are honestly a nice way to unwind. |

> Not really, no. I'd say talk shows can be genuinely entertaining, and a good host often sparks interesting debates about current issues. So you're relaxing and picking up new viewpoints at the same time. To be fair, some are pure fluff with celebrity gossip, but the better ones are honestly a nice way to unwind.

**Q6. Do you think we can acquire knowledge from watching TV programs?**

| 句 | 英式中文珠子 | 范文英文 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | 绝对，我 觉得 是。 | Absolutely, I think so. |
| 2 | 纪录片 和 教育 频道 塞满 事实、（你 否则 永远 撞不见的），比如 历史 或 自然 怎么 运作。 | Documentaries and educational channels are packed with facts you'd never bump into otherwise, like history or how nature works. |
| 3 | 甚至 剧集 也 能 教 你 关于 其他 社会 和 生活 方式。 | Even dramas can teach you about other societies and ways of life. |
| 4 | 说句公道话，你 得 有选择性，因为 一大堆 节目 是 纯 花架子，但 那些 对的 真的 拓宽 你的 头脑。 | To be fair, you've got to be selective, since plenty of programmes are pure fluff, but the right ones really broaden your mind. |

> Absolutely, I think so. Documentaries and educational channels are packed with facts you'd never bump into otherwise, like history or how nature works. Even dramas can teach you about other societies and ways of life. To be fair, you've got to be selective, since plenty of programmes are pure fluff, but the right ones really broaden your mind.

**P3 句型/模板**：`reality shows are huge here` · `easy to follow after a long day` · `the lighthearted ones tend to pull in the biggest crowds` · `made foreign shows really accessible` / `with subtitles` · `a fresh perspective and a peek into other cultures` · `local programmes in their own language` · `without leaving home, which a zoo simply can't match`（which后置）· `wild behaviour you'd never see in a cage`（relative后置）· `nothing quite beats the excitement of seeing a real animal up close` · `break up a lesson` / `which really helps students stay focused`（which后置）· `an experiment that's hard to describe in words`（that后置）· `keep classes lively and engaging` · `sparks interesting debates about current issues` · `relaxing and picking up new viewpoints at the same time` · `pure fluff with celebrity gossip` · `packed with facts you'd never bump into otherwise`（relative后置）/ `how nature works`（embedded）· `broaden your mind`。

---

> **全 54 张收官（新01–新27 + 老01–老27）。** 每张 = P2 拆解表 + P2 整段 + 句型/模板 + 6 道 P3（各 拆解表 + 整段）+ P3 句型/模板。英文逐字取自 `examples/` 对应题（范文档，未降级）。用法见文件顶部说明。
